It records accounts,\ncategories, and cash transactions in a relational database, enforces atomic commands and\nnon-negative balances, and reports balances, category totals, and monthly totals. Every value is\ncomputed by a query, and every amount is stored as integer cents.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- Python 3.11 or newer (`python.md`): implementation language.\n- SQLite (`sqlite.md`): the database, reached only through the shipped persistence library. This\n  build has no PostgreSQL backend.\n- CLI (`cli.md`): the `ledger` executable at the application root.\n- pytest (`python.md`): the test runner behind `bin/test.sh`.\n- POSIX sh (`common.md`): `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader.\n\n## Foundation\n\n- The project root holds `pyproject.toml` declaring Python 3.11 or newer, pytest, and ruff.\n- `uv.lock` is committed beside `pyproject.toml`.\n- `.venv` is created with `uv venv`. Launchers in `bin/` activate it.\n- `pytest -q` is the project test runner.\n\n## Modules\n\n| Module | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `cli` | Entry `main(argv)`, command routing, usage listing, CSV output writer, operational error mapping to exit codes |\n| `database_location` | Resolves the database location from `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db` |\n| `persistence` (generated) | Store access through `open_database(dsn)` and `db.<table>.<verb>(...)` |\n| `command_transaction` | Runs each command inside one database transaction; rolls back on any failure |\n| `validation` | Codes, names, memos, amounts as integer cents, calendar dates |\n| `balance` | Non-negative balance check after a command's changes and before commit |\n| `loader` | Bulk CSV loads, all or nothing |\n| `accounts` | Account add, get, rename, delete |\n| `transactions` | Post, get, amend, delete, and the next-id rule |\n| `transfers` | Two-row transfer stored as `TRANSFER` rows, or neither |\n| `reports` | Balances, categories, and monthly reports computed by query |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Allowed access |\n|---|---|---|\n| Persistence | `persistence` package generated from `DATABASE.md` | Domain modules call `open_database(dsn)` and `db.<table>.<verb>(...)` only. No module imports a database driver. |\n| Configuration | `database_location` | Only module that reads `DATABASE_URL` and `DATABASE_PATH` |\n| File store | None | Input CSV files are read only by `loader` |\n| External service | None | No network access, except to a PostgreSQL server named by `DATABASE_URL`. This build rejects that DSN with exit 1. |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Amounts are stored and computed as integer cents. No floating-point type is used for money.\n- Every command runs in one transaction through `command_transaction`. Any failure rolls back all changes.\n- The balance rule runs after a command's changes and before commit, for every touched account.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file. Seed files reach the database only through `ledger load`.\n- Reports are computed from the database. No report answer is embedded in code.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arch-python-version ===\nIntent: The running interpreter is Python 3.11 or newer, as the Technology Stack requires.\n\nimport sys\n\nassert sys.version_info[:2] >= (3, 11)\n=== END AC arch-python-version ===\n\n=== AC arch-pyproject ===\nIntent: pyproject.toml exists at the project root and parses as TOML with a project table.\n\nimport tomllib\n\nwith open(\"pyproject.toml\", \"rb\") as handle:\n    data = tomllib.load(handle)\nassert isinstance(data[\"project\"], dict)\n=== END AC arch-pyproject ===\n\n=== AC arch-uv-lock ===\nIntent: uv.lock is committed at the project root beside pyproject.toml.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nassert Path(\"uv.lock\").is_file()\n=== END AC arch-uv-lock ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Do not modify, rewrite, or substitute any file under `sources/`.\n- Do not open a database driver outside the `persistence` package.\n- Do It owns the entry point, the\ntest fixture that runs the entry point in process, and the `ledger` launcher that delegates to it.\nIt does not route commands; command routing belongs to `FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md`.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `cli.main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int` returns an exit code.\n- `cli.testing.cli_runner(*args: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]` returns `(exit_code, stdout, stderr)`\n  without spawning a process.\n- `./ledger` is an executable file at the application root that delegates its arguments to `cli.main`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-main-callable ===\nIntent: The cli package exposes main with the single parameter argv.\n\nimport inspect\n\nimport cli\n\nassert callable(cli.main)\nassert list(inspect.signature(cli.main).parameters) == [\"argv\"]\n=== END AC cli-main-callable ===\n\n=== AC cli-runner-shape ===\nIntent: cli_runner returns a three-element tuple of an integer exit code and two text streams.\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nresult = cli_runner()\nassert isinstance(result, tuple)\ncode, out, err = result\nassert isinstance(code, int)\nassert isinstance(out, str)\nassert isinstance(err, str)\n=== END AC cli-runner-shape ===\n\n=== AC cli-launcher-executable ===\nIntent: The ledger launcher is an executable file at the application root.\n\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nlauncher = Path(\"ledger\")\nassert launcher.is_file()\nassert os.access(launcher, os.X_OK)\n=== END AC cli-launcher-executable ===\n\n=== AC cli-launcher-shebang ===\nIntent: The ledger launcher begins with an interpreter line.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfirst_line = Path(\"ledger\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()[0]\nassert first_line.startswith(\"#!\")\n=== END AC cli-launcher-shebang ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Do not spawn a subprocess in any criterion. Criteria call `cli_runner` or import the entry point.\n- The launcher contains no command logic; it only delegates to `cli.main`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Database-Location.md ===\n# FEATURE: Database Location\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Resolve the database location from DATABASE_URL, then DATABASE_PATH, then data/app.db, and reject PostgreSQL DSNs in this build. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Platform.md |\n| Provides    | resolve_database_location() |\n| Consumes    | open_database(dsn) |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nEvery command finds its database through this resolution. The resolved value is passed to\n`open_database(dsn)`. This feature does not open a connection.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `database_location.resolve_database_location() -> str` returns the location string.\n- `database_location.DatabaseLocationError` is raised for an unsupported DSN.\n\n## Resolution Rules\n\n1. If `DATABASE_URL` is set, it is used.\n2. Else, if `DATABASE_PATH` is set, it is used.\n3. Else, the value is `data/app.db`, which is relative to the working directory.\n4. A `sqlite://` prefix is removed. The remainder is the location.\n5. A value beginning `postgres://` or `postgresql://` raises `DatabaseLocationError`. The CLI\n   boundary maps that error to exit 1.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC dbloc-default ===\nIntent: With neither DATABASE_URL nor DATABASE_PATH set, the location is data/app.db.\n\nimport os\n\nfrom database_location import resolve_database_location\n\nkeys = (\"DATABASE_URL\", \"DATABASE_PATH\")\nsaved = {key: os.environ.pop(key, None) for key in keys}\ntry:\n    location = resolve_database_location()\nfinally:\n    for key, value in saved.items():\n        if value is not None:\n            os.environ[key] = value\nprint(location)\nassert location == \"data/app.db\"\n=== END AC dbloc-default ===\n\n=== AC dbloc-path-fallback ===\nIntent: With only DATABASE_PATH set, the location is that path.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom database_location import resolve_database_location\n\nkeys = (\"DATABASE_URL\", \"DATABASE_PATH\")\nsaved = {key: os.environ.pop(key, None) for key in keys}\ntry:\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        path = str(Path(tmp) / \"ledger.db\")\n        os.environ[\"DATABASE_PATH\"] = path\n        location = resolve_database_location()\nfinally:\n    for key, value in saved.items():\n        if value is not None:\n            os.environ[key] = value\nprint(location)\nassert location == path\n=== END AC dbloc-path-fallback ===\n\n=== AC dbloc-url-precedence ===\nIntent: When both DATABASE_URL and DATABASE_PATH are set, DATABASE_URL wins.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom database_location import resolve_database_location\n\nkeys = (\"DATABASE_URL\", \"DATABASE_PATH\")\nsaved = {key: os.environ.pop(key, None) for key in keys}\ntry:\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        url = str(Path(tmp) / \"primary.db\")\n        other = str(Path(tmp) / \"secondary.db\")\n        os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = url\n        os.environ[\"DATABASE_PATH\"] = other\n        location = resolve_database_location()\nfinally:\n    for key, value in saved.items():\n        if value is not None:\n            os.environ[key] = value\nprint(location)\nassert location == url\n=== END AC dbloc-url-precedence ===\n\n=== AC dbloc-sqlite-prefix ===\nIntent: A sqlite:// prefix on DATABASE_URL is removed, leaving the file path.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom database_location import resolve_database_location\n\nkeys = (\"DATABASE_URL\", \"DATABASE_PATH\")\nsaved = {key: os.environ.pop(key, None) for key in keys}\ntry:\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        path = str(Path(tmp) / \"prefixed.db\")\n        os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = \"sqlite://\" + path\n        location = resolve_database_location()\nfinally:\n    for key, value in saved.items():\n        if value is not None:\n            os.environ[key] = value\nprint(location)\nassert location == path\n=== END AC BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: Cash Ledger\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Modules, boundaries, and technology stack for the `ledger` cash-ledger command-line program. |\n| Provides    | pyproject.toml, uv.lock, .venv, bin/ launchers |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe application is one executable named `ledger` at the application root. It records accounts,\ncategories, and cash transactions in a relational database, enforces atomic commands and\nnon-negative balances, and reports balances, category totals, and monthly totals. Every value is\ncomputed by a query, and every amount is stored as integer cents.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- Python 3.11 or newer (`python.md`): implementation language.\n- SQLite (`sqlite.md`): the database, reached only through the shipped persistence library. This\n  build has no PostgreSQL backend.\n- CLI (`cli.md`): the `ledger` executable at the application root.\n- pytest (`python.md`): the test runner behind `bin/test.sh`.\n- POSIX sh (`common.md`): `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader.\n\n## Foundation\n\n- The project root holds `pyproject.toml` declaring Python 3.11 or newer, pytest, and ruff.\n- `uv.lock` is committed beside `pyproject.toml`.\n- `.venv` is created with `uv venv`. Launchers in `bin/` activate it.\n- `pytest -q` is the project test runner.\n\n## Modules\n\n| Module | Responsibility |\n|---|---|\n| `cli` | Entry `main(argv)`, command routing, usage listing, CSV output writer, operational error mapping to exit codes |\n| `database_location` | Resolves the database location from `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db` |\n| `persistence` (generated) | Store access through `open_database(dsn)` and `db.<table>.<verb>(...)` |\n| `command_transaction` | Runs each command inside one database transaction; rolls back on any failure |\n| `validation` | Codes, names, memos, amounts as integer cents, calendar dates |\n| `balance` | Non-negative balance check after a command's changes and before commit |\n| `loader` | Bulk CSV loads, all or nothing |\n| `accounts` | Account add, get, rename, delete |\n| `transactions` | Post, get, amend, delete, and the next-id rule |\n| `transfers` | Two-row transfer stored as `TRANSFER` rows, or neither |\n| `reports` | Balances, categories, and monthly reports computed by query |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owner | Allowed access |\n|---|---|---|\n| Persistence | `persistence` package generated from `DATABASE.md` | Domain modules call `open_database(dsn)` and `db.<table>.<verb>(...)` only. No module imports a database driver. |\n| Configuration | `database_location` | Only module that reads `DATABASE_URL` and `DATABASE_PATH` |\n| File store | None | Input CSV files are read only by `loader` |\n| External service | None | No network access, except to a PostgreSQL server named by `DATABASE_URL`. This build rejects that DSN with exit 1. |\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Amounts are stored and computed as integer cents. No floating-point type is used for money.\n- Every command runs in one transaction through `command_transaction`. Any failure rolls back all changes.\n- The balance rule runs after a command's changes and before commit, for every touched account.\n- Application code never reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file. Seed files reach the database only through `ledger load`.\n- Reports are computed from the database. No report answer is embedded in code.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC arch-python-version ===\nIntent: The running interpreter is Python 3.11 or newer, as the Technology Stack requires.\n\nimport sys\n\nassert sys.version_info[:2] >= (3, 11)\n=== END AC arch-python-version ===\n\n=== AC arch-pyproject ===\nIntent: pyproject.toml exists at the project root and parses as TOML with a project table.\n\nimport tomllib\n\nwith open(\"pyproject.toml\", \"rb\") as handle:\n    data = tomllib.load(handle)\nassert isinstance(data[\"project\"], dict)\n=== END AC arch-pyproject ===\n\n=== AC arch-uv-lock ===\nIntent: uv.lock is committed at the project root beside pyproject.toml.\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nassert Path(\"uv.lock\").is_file()\n=== END AC arch-uv-lock ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Do not modify, rewrite, or substitute any file under `sources/`.\n- Do not open a database driver outside the `persistence` package.\n- Do not use floating point for money at any layer.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT DATABASE.md ===\n# DATABASE: Cash Ledger Store\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version Criteria call `cli_runner` or import the entry point.\n- The launcher contains no command logic; it only delegates to `cli.main`.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Database-Location.md ===\n# FEATURE: Database Location\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Resolve the database location from DATABASE_URL, then DATABASE_PATH, then data/app.db, and reject PostgreSQL DSNs in this build. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, DATABASE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Platform.md |\n| Provides    | resolve_database_location() |\n| Consumes    | open_database(dsn) |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nEvery command finds its database through this resolution. The resolved value is passed to\n`open_database(dsn)`. This feature does not open a connection.\n\n## Interface\n\n- `database_location.resolve_database_location() -> str` returns the location string.\n- `database_location.DatabaseLocationError` is raised for an unsupported DSN.\n\n## Resolution Rules\n\n1. If `DATABASE_URL` is set, it is used.\n2. Else, if `DATABASE_PATH` is set, it is used.\n3. Else, the value is `data/app.db`, which is relative to the working directory.\n4. A `sqlite://` prefix is removed. The remainder is the location.\n5. A value beginning `postgres://` or `postgresql://` raises `DatabaseLocationError`. The CLI\n   boundary maps that error to exit 1.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC dbloc-default ===\nIntent: With neither DATABASE_URL nor DATABASE_PATH set, the location is data/app.db.\n\nimport os\n\nfrom database_location import resolve_database_location\n\nkeys = (\"DATABASE_URL\", \"DATABASE_PATH\")\nsaved = {key: os.environ.pop(key, None) for key in keys}\ntry:\n    location = resolve_database_location()\nfinally:\n    for key, value in saved.items():\n        if value is not None:\n            os.environ[key] = value\nprint(location)\nassert location == \"data/app.db\"\n=== END AC dbloc-default ===\n\n=== AC dbloc-path-fallback ===\nIntent: With only DATABASE_PATH set, the location is that path.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom database_location import resolve_database_location\n\nkeys = (\"DATABASE_URL\", \"DATABASE_PATH\")\nsaved = {key: os.environ.pop(key, None) for key in keys}\ntry:\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        path = str(Path(tmp) / \"ledger.db\")\n        os.environ[\"DATABASE_PATH\"] = path\n        location = resolve_database_location()\nfinally:\n    for key, value in saved.items():\n        if value is not None:\n            os.environ[key] = value\nprint(location)\nassert location == path\n=== END AC dbloc-path-fallback ===\n\n=== AC dbloc-url-precedence ===\nIntent: When both DATABASE_URL and DATABASE_PATH are set, DATABASE_URL wins.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom database_location import resolve_database_location()\nfinally:\n    for key, value in saved.items():\n        if value is not None:\n            os.environ[key] = value\nprint(location)\nassert location == url\n=== END AC dbloc-url-precedence ===\n\n=== AC dbloc-sqlite-prefix ===\nIntent: A sqlite:// prefix on DATABASE_URL is removed, leaving the file path.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import dbloc-sqlite-prefix ===\n\n=== AC dbloc-postgres-rejected ===\nIntent: postgres:// and postgresql:// DSNs raise DatabaseLocationError in this build.\n\nimport os\n\nfrom database_location import == 2\n=== END AC dbloc-postgres-rejected ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- This feature reads the environment only. It never opens a database connection.\n- Application code outside this feature never reads `DATABASE_URL` or `DATABASE_PATH` directly.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Operational-Errors.md ===\n# FEATURE: Operational Errors\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261020 V1 |\n| Description | Map unreachable databases, missing tables, and unreadable input files to exit 1 with no change. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-Database-Location.md, FEATURE-CLI-Dispatch.md |\n| Consumes    | resolve_database_location() |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nOperational failures are distinct from usage errors and rejections. When the database cannot be\nreached, a non-`init` command finds its tables missing, or an input file cannot be read, the\ncommand exits 1, writes nothing to standard output, writes one diagnostic line to standard error,\nand changes nothing.\n\n## Interface\n\n- Exit code `1` for every operational failure.\n- Standard output is empty on failure.\n- Standard error carries exactly one non-empty diagnostic line.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC dberr-unreachable ===\nIntent: A database location under a missing directory exits 1 with empty stdout and one stderr line.\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    dsn = str(Path(tmp) / \"absent-dir\" / \"ledger.db\")\n    saved = os.environ.get(\"DATABASE_URL\")\n    os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = dsn\n    try:\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(\"count\")\n    finally:\n        if saved is None:\n            os.environ.pop(\"DATABASE_URL\", None)\n        else:\n            os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = saved\nprint(out)\nprint(err, file=sys.stderr)\nlines = [line for line in err.splitlines() if line.strip()]\nassert code == 1\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len(lines) == 1\n=== END AC dberr-unreachable ===\n\n=== AC dberr-missing-tables ===\nIntent: A non-init command against an empty database with no tables exits 1 with empty stdout.\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    db = Path(tmp) / \"empty.db\"\n    db.touch()\n    saved = os.environ.get(\"DATABASE_URL\")\n    os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = str(db)\n    try:\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(\"count\")\n    finally:\n        if saved is None:\n            os.environ.pop(\"DATABASE_URL\", None)\n        else:\n            os.environ[\"DATABASE_URL\"] = saved\nprint(out)\nprint(err, file=sys.stderr)\nlines = [line for line in err.splitlines() if line.strip()]\nassert code == 1\nassert out == \"\"\nassert len(lines) == 1\n=== END AC dberr-missing-tables ===\n\n=== AC dberr-unreadable-input ===\nIntent: A load whose CSV file does not exist exits 1 with empty stdout.\n\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tempfile\nfrom pathlib import
